- **faostat-infographic**: Use when the user wants a modern, non-expert-facing visual summary of FAOSTAT data on a single page — an infographic, one-pager, visual summary, explainer card, or shareable graphic for social, pitch decks, or press use. Aesthetic — Visual Capitalist / Our World in Data explainer cards / Statista — bold typography, generous whitespace, one hero stat, iconography over chart axes.
